Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The arima operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 184. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 580 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the arima space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 184 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 580. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 220.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the arima structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 171. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 746 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
